▶ END-TO-END-RUN §3 — deterministic behavior
  ✔ §3 New received 10:00, actual no-answer call 10:10: first response satisfied by 10:30; one ordinary credit; initiator goal credit (10.443ms)
  ✔ §3 Actual retry 10:20, next call 11:10: retry earns goal only, no anchor reset; 11:10 satisfies the second call; SMS still owed (2.8082ms)
  ✔ §3 New Day 1/3/4/5/6 and SMS Day 6/9: age-based quotas/deadlines; fixed SMS dates unaffected by extra/missed sends (7.4763ms)
  ✔ §3 Receipt/return at 18:00: inclusive caps, no manufactured earlier miss (10.6911ms)
  ✔ §3 Receipt/return at 19:30: inclusive caps, no manufactured earlier miss (3.6912ms)
  ✔ §3 Receipt/return at 19:31: inclusive caps, no manufactured earlier miss (4.604ms)
  ✔ §3 Closing/midnight crossing: outbound start date governs the goal; no future quota prepayment (4.5101ms)
  ✔ §3 Originating answered inbound Call Lead: initial response + at most one arrival call, zero outbound goal (2.7547ms)
  ✔ §3 Helpers/transfers/duplicate receipts: one canonical credit, initiator-only goal, no assignment widening (1.6006ms)
  ✔ §3 SMS sent then confirmed failure: credit revoked with bounded recomputation; no Call/goal change (2.7834ms)
  ✔ §3 Unverified identity/association or capture gap: pending, no guessed credit or false zero/failure (5.5354ms)
  ✔ §3 Blank/malformed priority update and valid change: retain verified policy with uncertainty; accepted change supersedes once (7.5675ms)
  ✔ §3 Quoted date/no-date/reentry: working-date selection/default, original-age reentry, no restarted initial clock (12.1859ms)
  ✔ §3 Three unsuccessful attempts: advisory warning only; restrictions still block; restricted outbound earns zero (3.1036ms)
  ✔ §3 Restriction starts/releases: affected-channel waiver, no prohibited-interval debt, earlier misses kept, paused initial minutes, release-date rule (6.9018ms)
